## *Sixth & final period.*

The last door has closed upon the being, like all those he has already passed. Thrust upon a dark, sterile, & icy road, he may still drag himself along for a long time, struggling constantly against the infirmities expressed by the frosts of AQUARIUS (♒). Finally, on the threshold of the funerary domain of LETHOPHORO (♄) <note>original: "Léthophoro." Likely derived from the Greek for "bearer of forgetfulness" (Lethe), here associated with Saturn (♄), the planet traditionally linked to old age, time, and death.</note>, the being finds a skiff in the shape of a tomb; he lies down in it, overwhelmed; putting himself at the mercy of the black wave of the Lethe <note>In Greek mythology, the Lethe is the river of forgetfulness in the underworld.</note>, designated by the *Pisces* (♓) & which, rocking him more or less stormily, finally brings him to that devouring repository where every being must come to strip themselves of their material elements. — Thus ends, in the 37th square <note>The journey ends on the 37th square of the game board, completing the allegorical path of a human life.</note>, the sixth and final period of life.

### *Division of a Woman's Life.*

As for this charming and frail sex, whose moral essence the Creator was pleased to sublimate at the expense of physical substance, the **GREAT KABBALAH** <term>Grande Cabale</term> <note>In this context, the "Kabbalah" refers to a mystical or esoteric system of numerology and symbolic calculation used by the author to map out human existence.</note> establishes the following difference. A woman’s life path is likewise divided into six periods, subject to the same **INTELLIGENCES** <note>Spiritual or celestial entities believed to govern specific stages of human life.</note> and containing the same signs; but whereas for a man, all the compartments of the zodiacal zone are equal and each counts for one <term>lustrum</term> <note>A period of five years.</note>; for a woman, the first twelve compartments (that is, from the 20th to the 31st inclusive) each count for only four years, so that upon leaving the 9th compartment (the 28th of the **GREAT MIRROR** <note>Likely a reference to a specific diagram or symbolic chart within this esoteric system.</note>), the woman is still only 36 years old. The **GREAT KABBALAH** assigns this age to her as a maturity equal to that which a man is thought to reach only at 45. — At the end of the 4th epoch, a woman, according to the **GREAT KABBALAH**, completes her 48th year. This is the age when Nature ceases to regard her as useful for its primary aims <note>A reference to the end of the reproductive years.</note>; it is thus the true point where *decrepitude* [*vétusté*] <note>The original French term "vétusté" suggests a state of being worn by time or old age.</note> begins. ## SIXTH STEP.

**It** leads into the knowledge of the *temporal regime*, or *chronological*, nearly as far as the preceding step led into the knowledge of the *sidereal regime* <term>Sidereal regime: The study or governance of celestial bodies and stars over human life and nature.</term>.

## Of the Great Dial

We mentioned above that under the *chronological system* <note>original: "régime chronique"; a system for organizing or governing time</note> the zones are legends with a double purpose. Here is what this means.

The focal point and the zones—by means of a dual attribution (that is, through an *expansion from the center toward the circumference*, and a *contraction from the circumference toward the center*)—become capable of different meanings. There are thus two movements assigned to the temporal or chronological system: the *eccentric* and the *concentric*.

### Eccentric Movement

In this hypothesis, the focal point or center (1) represents *one thousand years* and is called the *millenary square*. The six surrounding it represent as many centuries. In this regard, the planets, leaving the SIDEREAL zone, have come to occupy the *cen-* <note>The word is interrupted by a page break; it likely continues as "central" or "centrale" on the next page.</note>

->D 3<-

The author defines the center of this system as a "millenary square" representing a thousand years, surrounded by squares representing centuries.</summary>

In this case, it is named the *Climacteric Zone*. ### *Concentric Movement.*

### Finding the day of the month.

There are two ways to reach this goal.
The first: Keeping 31 *pieces* <note>These are the numbered tokens or tiles drawn from the urn.</note> (because March has 31 days), the rest are returned to the urn. Once this is done, the last piece is lifted—(*here one must refer back to the table of its formation, Plate I*)—and one says: *fifteen*. If the revealed piece is 66, the marriage will take place on the 15th of March. If not, the next piece is lifted, and one says: *fourteen*; continuing until 66 finally appears. One then knows on which day of the first division <note>The first half of the month (days 1 through 15).</note> of the month the marriage will be; if not, and all pieces have been lifted in vain, the marriage is for the second division.

### *Observation regarding this process.*

It naturally occurs to the mind that since a climacteric dial <note>A "dial" here refers to the geometric arrangement of tokens or pieces used in this divination system.</note> only covers a span of forty-nine years, might the friend live longer? ### *General Instruction.*

Before establishing a figure, the question must be clearly framed, and its *interesting ramifications* anticipated: then, preference is given to the figure that is most suitable: the *small triangle* for 3; the *small diamond* for 4; the *same* for 5 (with a spare piece).

### *Advice.*

This is why I advise the Candidate who is going to apply themselves to the study of the **GREAT KABBALAH** <note>original: "GRANDE CABALE"; here referring to a specific system of occult calculation or numerology used for divination</note>, never to go to the trouble of investigations without having ensured whether they have a stable foundation in the seeker's state of being. One risks nothing, for example, by consulting our **KABBALAH** for a sick person, concerning *their death* or *their return to health*; on the *success* or *failure of the hopes* of a man to whom possible chances would ensure an inheritance; or on the future marriage of a bachelor not bound by vows <note>referring to religious or monastic vows of celibacy</note>, or that of people freed by widowhood. But if it pleased, for example, a Jew to inquire if he is to one day become Pope? Although, among so many billions of extraordinary chances that the bizarre nature of the social mechanism can produce, it might finally be possible for this Jew to obtain the tiara of the Christian Pontiff, there would nevertheless be almost as much folly in the Diviner as in the Israelite himself to cabalistically stir up this question. ### *Essential Observation.*

Just as the solar and lunar signs mark the exact points of midday and midnight, each hourly number marks the precise middle of the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, etc. fragment, or twelfth <note>original: "douzieme" — likely referring to the tradition of 'unequal' or planetary hours, where day and night are each divided into 12 equal parts regardless of the season</note> of the day or night. Failing to pay attention to this would lead to frequent errors, sometimes by as much as half an hour.

The convenience and opportunities for using the small figures cannot be confined within the framework of any single rule. ### *Variation.*

In the table of principles—Spirits, Intelligences, and Numbers—one sees: 1st. that at the beginning and end of each row <term>assise: a horizontal layer or row in a table</term> of the nine columns, there is one of the 12 signs of the zodiac. 2nd. That after seven columns, starting from no. 1, there is a double perpendicular line which seems to set apart the last two. (*See Plate II.*)

According to the initiate whom we have just cited, the first seven **SIGNS** and **NUMBERS** of each **ROW** divide among themselves the entire duration of the sun's stay in each marginal zodiacal sign; in such a way that the Sun (☉), in Aries (♈), represents all Sundays; the Moon (☽) all Mondays; Venus (♀) all Fridays; Mercury (☿) all Wednesdays; Saturn (♄) all Saturdays; Jupiter (♃) all Thursdays; and Mars (♂) all Tuesdays. In such a way also that the number or figure which has served as the indicator of a fortune <note>original: "chance" — in this context, a predicted outcome or a lucky event identified through the system</note> and of the year in which it must take place, also designates the month and the day of the week. Therefore, it is then only a matter of learning which of the four (or five) such days that occur in the announced month will be the one on which the predicted fortune will be fixed...

<summary>This page describes a "Variation" in the divinatory system, explaining how the Table of Principles uses zodiac signs and planetary symbols to pinpoint the specific year, month, and day of a predicted event.</summary>
<keywords>astrological timing, planetary days, zodiac signs, Table of Principles, divination methods, Spirits and Intelligences</keywords>

( 92 )

...fixed. To this end, according to the procedure indicated on page 72, the "speaking number" is shuffled <term>balotte: the act of drawing or mixing lots (from 'ballot') to randomize a selection</term> with three (or four) others, drawn randomly from the urn. Depending on the position that the expressive number holds within the *small lozenge*, or outside it, one learns whether it is the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, or 5th instance of the given day of the *week* that the sought-after chance will take place. The rank of the *weekly* day determines the date <term>quantième: the specific numerical day of the month, such as the 14th or 22nd</term> of the month.

As for the hours, our same initiate (regardless of the *solar* or *lunar* signs intended to mark the exact points of *midday* and *midnight*) regarded the first hour of the day or night as that which, in the *solar* or *lunar* columns, represents the number to which the zodiac sign is attached: so that, during the entire duration of the sun's stay in *Aries* (♈), the *solar* or *lunar* signs themselves were those of the first hour of day or night: consequently 1 and 9 indicated the first hour of day, 2 and 8 the first hour of night; under Taurus (♉), 10 and 18 the first hour of day; 11 and 17 the first hour of night under Gemini (♊): and so on, descending until Pisces (♓), under which 9 and 99 represent the first *solar* or daytime hour; 92 and 98 the first *lunar* or nighttime hour. (Put this into practice.)

<summary>This page explains how to pinpoint the exact day and hour of a predicted event by shuffling numbers and using the sun's position in the zodiac to identify the "first hour" of a given period.</summary>

<meta>The previous page detailed specific calculations for timing events based on the zodiac. This page offers a philosophical and methodological reflection on those practices, emphasizing the role of divine guidance.</meta>

## Observation.

We do not decide which is better: this latter method or the one we have indicated elsewhere. We believe that they are equally good, since we have seen skilled people practice with success using both. Being familiar with them will at least provide a means to vary the operations, and consequently to verify and refine the *results*. The fact that certain procedures may seem arbitrary leads the virtuous Kabbalist <term>Cabaliſte: here referring to a practitioner of "Lottery Kabbalah," a system of mystical numerology used to predict winning numbers or future events</term> to think that, provided he understands himself well, *it is the PURE SPIRIT who does the rest, directing, by its omnipotence, the order of numbers in the MIRRORS <term>MIROIRS: numerical tables or magic squares used in divination to "reflect" truths</term>, according to the meaning it wishes the practitioner to find there.* Without this *faith*, there is no connection between the SUPREME BEING and man: and consequently, no GREAT CABALA.

But the confidence that the KABBALIST must have in the aid of the PURE SPIRIT must not excuse him from operating with the most religious attention, nor from using all the means his knowledge might suggest to approach the exact truth as closely as possible through various paths. There are finicky <note>original: "vétilleux," implying cases involving small, tedious, or overly-detailed points</note> cases which can nonetheless be very important. The Kabbalist must take great care not to rely immediately on the pre-

<summary>This page argues that while multiple Kabbalistic methods may be valid, the practitioner's success depends on a combination of rigorous technical attention and faith in a divine "Pure Spirit" that organizes the numbers.</summary>
<keywords>methodology, Kabbalist, Pure Spirit, Mirrors, Supreme Being, Great Cabala, faith vs. technique, numerology</keywords>

# SECOND SUPPLEMENT.

---

**A** glorious destination that the Editor of these cabalistic notions <note>cabalistic: refers to the Kabbalah, a tradition of Jewish mysticism often adapted by European occultists to find hidden meanings in numbers and letters.</note> has in view for them, requires that he leave as little as possible to be desired by the Curious <note>the Curious: a term often used in this period to describe scholars, collectors, and seekers of esoteric knowledge.</note> who will share the fifty copies to which the printing of the *Telescope of Zoroaster* <note>Telescope of Zoroaster: the title of this specific divinatory or philosophical system, named after the ancient Persian prophet often associated with magic in Western occultism.</note> must be limited. We are therefore going to open one more window. From there, for certain, the gaze of the being to whom the Pure-Spirit has given the eyes one must have to discover certain sublime objects; from there, we say, & by means of this second supplement, the gaze of the **CHOSEN ONE** <note>original: "ELU." ### **CABALISTIC ANGELS.**

Just as the intelligent & faithful dog is to the Shepherd—to whom nature has given the instinct to direct, at his Master's will and according to all the rules of the most prudent tactics, a flock less enlightened and less diligent than himself—various **Angels** do the same for the nine superior **Intelligences** <note>In many occult systems, "Intelligences" refer to the conscious spiritual entities that govern the movements and influences of the planets.</note> described in the third **Step** or **Chapter** of this work.

The **Great Cabala** <note>original: "Grande Cabale." The Cabala (or Kabbalah) is a tradition of Jewish mysticism that was heavily adapted by Christian and Hermetic scholars during the Renaissance and Enlightenment to explain the structure of the universe.</note> recognizes, as we have seen, nine **Intelligences**; but the two *solar* ones, as well as the two *lunar* ones, correspond as a pair to only a single *planet*; it may even be that the *material* intelligence, subjected to each of the two great **Luminaries** <note>original: "Astres." In astrology, the Sun and Moon are often called the "Luminaries" rather than planets.</note>, is in some way a primary satellite of the *spiritual* intelligence, rather than an **Intelligence** of the first order. On this basis, the totality of the **Intelligences** has only seven *foundations*, which constitute the seven *Planets*. Each of these *Planets* has four *Messengers*, (**Angels**, according to the Greek etymology, *Angelos* <note>original: "Αγγελοσ." ...know) influences all humans born under *it*; that is to say, during the reign it holds, which is 13 days, 61 minutes, and 25 seconds per year. <note>The author’s calculation uses a 28-star system. 13 days multiplied by 28 stars equals 364 days; the additional minutes and seconds account for the remaining time needed to reach the solar year.</note> In such a way, each total of the reigns of the 28 **Birth Stars**—anticipating by 6 hours per year the totality of 365 days—allows the 366th day of the **leap year** to be encompassed within the net total of days for four years. ## D E T A I L E D   E X P L A N A T I O N .

The preceding Table does not offer any of the simple numbers (that is to say, from 1 to 9 inclusive); this is because these numbers, as has already been stated elsewhere, belong specifically to the **INTELLIGENCES** <note>In this occult system, "Intelligences" refers to a rank of spiritual beings superior to Angels, representing the core essence or "brain" of a celestial sphere.</note> themselves, of which they are the respective representatives. On this subject, refer back to the third Step <note>The author uses "Steps" to denote chapters or levels of initiation.</note>, in the article entitled: **Numbers assigned to the INTELLIGENCES.**

Neither does the Table make mention of all the squares of the **GREAT MIRROR** <note>The "Great Mirror" likely refers to a specific 37-square magical grid or talismanic diagram central to this manuscript's practice.</note>; there are 37 of them: the **ANGELS** occupy only 28; the nine others serve as the seats of the **INTELLIGENCES** themselves, which, numbering nine, complete (with the 28) this number of 37, which is the total of the *squares*.

The center 1 is occupied by **PSYKELIA** <note>Greek root: *Psyche*, meaning soul or breath.</note>.
The 3rd square is occupied by **GENHELIA** <note>Greek root: *Genos*, meaning birth or origin.</note>.
The —— 6th — by **PSYCHOMENA** <note>Greek root: *Psychomene*, perhaps meaning "tempered soul."</note>.
The —— 9th — by **ADAMASTO** <note>Greek root: *Adamastos*, meaning untamable or invincible.</note>.
The —— 11th — by **EROSIA** <note>Greek root: *Eros*, meaning desire or love.</note>.
The —— 13th — by **AGLAE** <note>Greek root: *Aglaia*, meaning splendor or beauty.</note>.
The —— 15th — by **PANURGIO** <note>Greek root: *Panourgos*, meaning all-working or resourceful/cunning.</note>:
The —— 17th — by **SELENO** <note>Greek root: *Selene*, the Moon.</note>.
The —— 19th — by **LETHOPHORO** <note>Greek root: *Lethe* (forgetfulness) and *phoros* (bearing); the "bringer of oblivion."</note>.

This is the moment that the *Spirit*—who deigns to communicate with this privileged mortal—duly causes to be seized. It is for this mortal to know how to profit from the distinguished favor bestowed upon him; and not to confuse with a *dream* that which may be *revelation*, *inspiration*, and sometimes *apparition*. # EXPLANATION

-> *Concerning the large dial*, the **NATAL STARS**, & the **ANGELS** *who govern them*. <-

In this dial, the *Tympanum* <note>original: "Tympan." In the context of dials or clocks, this refers to the face or the inner surface of the instrument.</note> & the *Border* must be considered separately.

The *Tympanum* is a large hexagon of 37 pieces, similar to the **GRAND MIRROR**, Pl. 3. & to the large hourly dial, Pl. 4.

This **LARGE HEXAGON** presents all the **INTELLIGENCES** <note>In Western esotericism, "Intelligences" are spiritual beings or consciousnesses associated with planets or spheres, ranking higher than ordinary spirits but often serving as intermediaries.</note> & all the **ANGELS**, named and placed, each in their designated spot. The Arabic numeral seen at the top of each name indicates the rank held, in the **GRAND MIRROR**, by the *cell* where this name is written. Below the names of **INTELLIGENCES**, one sees, as in the **GRAND MIRROR**, the *planetary sign*, and, in the rays cast by this sign, one can distinguish, in very small...

Angel, who represents it, occupies in the GRAND MIRROR.

In this manner, the **Tympanum** <note>The inner face or "drum" of the dial.</note> and the border mutually provide the necessary information so that by glancing at any name, one immediately knows to which *Angel* a *Star* belongs, or this *Star* to an *Angel*, what the rank is, and which cell this inseparable pair occupies in the GRAND MIRROR <note>The "Grand Mirror" likely refers to a large symbolic or mathematical table that serves as the key to the entire system.</note>.

Finally, the outermost band will be divided only by 365 points at equal distances, representing the 365 days of the year; this provides 13 *points* under each division of twenty-eight, except for the fraction of six hours per year, which encompasses one day after four years. On this basis, during the first year following a *leap year* <note>original: "bissextile." From the Latin *bis sextus*, referring to the doubling of the sixth day before the Calends of March in the Roman calendar.</note>, each point—starting from the first, beginning from the western horizon—advances by 12 minutes and 25 seconds over the next point, which results in a total of 6 hours for all points collectively at the end of a year.

Thus, in the following year, the duration of each space represented by a division of 365 is only 18 hours for the first day, with the remainder advancing to the next point. The same applies to the third and fourth year, where (as we have said) the period is completed by eliminating the fraction caused by a 366th day.

This meticulous detail will only feel cumbersome in cases where a precise moment must be sought very close to the instant of the transition from the reign of one *natal star* <note>The star believed to govern a specific period of time or an individual's destiny at birth.</note> to that of the next. But nothing is gained without labor: and even if we could make the definition of the process for finding the junction between one of these reigns and the next even clearer, we would take care not to do so, in order to force the CANDIDATE <note>The student or initiate practicing this art.</note> to search for it themselves. This is the true way to *know well* and to be *sure*: one is never truly certain when they have based an operation on the routine calculation of a set table <note>original: "tarif." In this context, a pre-calculated reference sheet or scale used to bypass manual work.</note>.

## THIRD SUPPLEMENT.

In this singular production, at once too long and too short, which we launch into the whirlwind of the world at the risk of seeing it fall into certain hands where it will be either useless or clumsily employed; we could not have more accurately found a middle ground between the danger of saying *too much* and that of saying *too little*. On one hand, the individual who wishes to apply themselves to the study of an Art or a science becomes lazy as soon as they expect to receive, in a perfectly distilled form, the doctrine for which they thirst. However, in matters of the <term>Great Cabala</term> <note>A system of mystical or esoteric knowledge, often involving complex calculations and spiritual interpretations of the universe.</note>, the instructor is not permitted to deliver anything beyond the ingredients *in their natural state*, the *tools, and the indication of the process necessary for the preparation of the specific* <note>original: "spécifique." In alchemy and traditional medicine, a 'specific' is a remedy intended for a particular purpose; here it refers to the spiritual or mathematical result of the work.</note>. On the other hand, the individual who is left alone in the midst of scattered provisions, without having been set to work, may either become discouraged if they are lukewarm and irresolute, or go astray if they are ardent and dominated by a presumptuous self-love. To these two general inconveniences which we cite, and whose variations are infinite, we know of no sure remedy. It us

<meta>The catchword "Il nous" (It us) suggests the sentence continues on the next page, likely as "It remains for us..." or "It seems to us..."</meta>

<summary>The author justifies his pedagogical approach to the "Great Cabala," arguing that an instructor must only provide the tools and methods, as providing a complete solution would make the student lazy or prone to error.</summary>
<keywords>Great Cabala, pedagogy, esoteric education, specific remedy, student discipline, occult philosophy</keywords>

only remains for us to repeat here that *everything that was appropriate to say has been said;* and that it is absolutely necessary for each person to discover the rest, or humbly renounce the *cabalistic science* <note>A system of mystical knowledge involving the interpretation of hidden meanings in numbers and sacred texts.</note>. Let us try, however, to condense (to conclude) the entire scope of this most immense path into a framework where the objects are brought together as clearly as possible.

### Syntheses.

1st. There is therefore no **Kabbalist** who should pride himself on being an improviser: the wisest is he who, even when an expressive combination of numbers strikes him, doubts until the entire *mirror* has been scrupulously analyzed, and all the chances have been verified.

### *Means.*

For this purpose, it is advisable to obtain the means to fix the *figures* and *numbers*, given that the slightest thing can disturb the *tableau* <note>The "tableau" is the visual arrangement or "picture" of the results.</note> formed by the *pieces*, as they are drawn from the *urn*. One may, to this end, use a small board slightly hollowed out, where each *piece* can fit into a place made for it: or else the board may be pierced with holes, according to the *series* of a *large mirror*, and receive numbered <term>pegs<note>original: "fichets"; small markers or pins used to record results on a game-like board.</note></term> corresponding to the pieces that have come out of the urn: or else one may have drawn or printed tables, where all the *hexagons* will be blank, and where one will write, according to the defined *procedure* of the drawing, the *figures* and *numbers* that the *urn* has provided.

One or the other of these precautions being taken, one is

The <term>Kabbalist: A practitioner of mystical interpretation and occult arts.</term> by thus fixing the *figures & numbers* of all the *tablets* <note>original: "tableaux"; these are the charts or layouts of symbols resulting from the divination.</note> which he has practiced, provides himself with as many *studies*, from which he can—if warned in time—recognize whether he performed *well or poorly*, the event having *justified* or *disproven his prophecies*. Furthermore, he will be quite astonished to see, in the long run, within more than one *tablet*, things which at the time he had not the sagacity to discover.

Now, these favorable or unfavorable qualities of each *Angel* are very clearly deduced by means of Plate VI, which indicates the planets and the numbers, as well as the place that each governs within a great mirror.

### *Second Warning.*

An ANGEL presides over the moment of a *birth*; (See the dial of the *Natal Stars*,) but two <term>Intelligences: Celestial beings or spirits that are higher or more abstract than individual angels.</term>, ordinarily, concur to animate, within the maternal womb, the individual who is to be born. To this end, their *chosen Angels* each release a spark of the fire which is their own, & this explosion occurs through the material vehicle of the two earthly individuals who cooperate in bringing a third out of nothingness... Woe to the creature for whom two evil *Angels* have provided the *spiritual element*. Happiness for the one who has been animated by two *Angels* of the other nature. *Struggles*, *storms*, highs & lows for the *being* in whom good & evil spiritual influences are at odds. ### Conclusion.

Let us now resign ourselves to seeing only a very small number of privileged individuals derive any benefit from our *Cabalistic Rudiment* <note>original: "Rudiment cabalistique"; referring to the foundational principles of the Kabbalah, a school of Jewish mysticism adapted here for Western occultism.</note>. Let us, moreover, prepare ourselves to see the multitude of *fools*, *idiots*, and *fanatics* hurl stones—some in mockery, others spewing curses against the Editor (who remains fortunately imperturbable) of the TELESCOPE OF ZOROASTER <note>Zoroaster (or Zarathustra) was an ancient Persian prophet. In the 18th century, he was often reclaimed by occultists as a "master of magic" and wisdom.</note>.
